</h2>
<a href="/news/article-7604041/39-dead-bodies-container-Thurrock-police-say.html">
<img class="b-loaded" data-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/6781542224889491592/308x174_JPG_6781542224889491592.jpg" data-fps="7" data-frame-count="63" data-frame-height="172" data-frame-width="308" data-hd-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/6781542224889491592/636x382_JPG-HD_6781542224889491592.jpg" data-hd-fps="7" data-hd-frame-count="63" data-hd-frame-height="358" data-hd-frame-width="636" data-mol-fe-video-preview="" data-video="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/6781542224889491592/964x580_MP4_6781542224889491592.mp4" height="542" src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/6781542224889491592/964x580_JPG-SINGLE_6781542224889491592.jpg" width="964"/><video loop="" style="height: 542px; width: 964px; display: none;"><source src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/6781542224889491592/964x580_MP4_6781542224889491592.mp4" type="video/mp4"/></video>
<noscript>
<img height="542" src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/6781542224889491592/964x580_JPG-SINGLE_6781542224889491592.jpg" width="964">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div class="articletext">
<div>
<p>
        
        The bodies were found in a lorry on the Waterglade Industrial Park in Grays, at roughly 1.40am this morning. It is understood the lorry was originally from Bulgaria and was travelling from Holyhead, Wales, where ferries sail to and from Ireland. The lorry's driver, a 24-year-old man from Northern Ireland, has been arrested on suspicion of murder. Police have not said where they believed the people who died were from or where they were thought to be travelling to. They said it could take a long time to identify those who have died. Authorities were warned last year that people smugglers saw the Irish border as a weak point in the UK border and were taking migrants to Ireland before transporting them to Britain. It is not known whether that happened in this case.
      </p>
</div>
</div>

<div class="article article-large cleared" data-original-title="General election in December? Jeremy Corbyn tells Labour he will back snap poll" itemprop="itemListElement" itemscope="" itemtype="//schema.org/ListItem">
<meta content="2" itemprop="position"/>
<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/news/article-7603835/Is-Britain-heading-DECEMBER-election-Jeremy-Corbyn-tells-Labour-snap-poll.html" itemprop="url">Is Britain heading for a DECEMBER election? Jeremy Corbyn WILL back Boris Johnson's call for snap poll if EU grant a long delay - despite Labour's disastrous polls - as Downing Street goes into election mode</a>
</h2>
<a href="/news/article-7603835/Is-Britain-heading-DECEMBER-election-Jeremy-Corbyn-tells-Labour-snap-poll.html">
<img alt="General election in December? Jeremy Corbyn tells Labour he will back snap poll" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/10/article-7603835-20075276-482_308x454.jpg" height="454" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="308"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="General election in December? Jeremy Corbyn tells Labour he will back snap poll" height="454" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/10/article-7603835-20075276-482_308x454.jpg" width="308">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div class="articletext-holder">
<p>
      
      Labour leader Jeremy Corbyn is ready to back a general election despite dismal poll ratings, paving the way for a potential pre-Christmas vote. Boris Johnson (main picture) will push to go to the polls if Brussels follows Donald Tusk's advice to grant another Brexit delay, after MPs rejected the Prime Minister's three-day timetable for his Brexit bill. Mr Corbyn threw down the gauntlet to rebellious Remainers in his party who want a second referendum first, with one of his closest allies branding it 'fantasy politics' today. All eyes will now be on EU leaders amid signs of disunity over how long to extend the Brexit process, with France and Germany preferring a short extension of just weeks.
    </p>

<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/news/article-7602911/Furious-mother-three-slams-elderly-couple-refusing-seats-reserved.html" itemprop="url">Furious mother-of-three slams elderly couple for refusing to move from the seats she reserved for her and her children on a packed train (but what would you have done?)</a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/news/article-7602911/Furious-mother-three-slams-elderly-couple-refusing-seats-reserved.html">
<img alt="Furious mother-of-three slams elderly couple for refusing to move from the seats she" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/01/20063548-0-image-a-58_1571792249146.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="Furious mother-of-three slams elderly couple for refusing to move from the seats she" height="382" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/01/20063548-0-image-a-58_1571792249146.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div>
<p>
            
            A furious mother-of-three has slammed an elderly couple (right) for not moving from seats she had reserved for her and her family. Amanda Mancino-Williams claimed the couple refused to move after she advised them that she had pre-booked seats on the packed train from Cheltenham to Nottingham. The 37-year-old said the couple had 'posh accents' and said her reserved tickets 'didn't matter'. Once Amanda consulted the train conductor on Tuesday afternoon she was offered four seats in first class with her children. Her children (left) were forced to cram onto three seats. After posting the images on Twitter, Amanda received praise for her handling of the situation (bottom right) with some claiming the people were miserable (right).
        </p>
</div>

<img alt="" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/00/20060664-0-image-a-26_1571786308281.jpg" height="115" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="154"/>

<h6>
      MEGHAN &amp; HARRY DOCUMENTARY
  </h6>
<div> </div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="article article-tri-headline">
<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/femail/article-7601407/Meghan-Markle-arrives-public-appearance-ITV-documentary-Royal-Albert-Hall.html">Meghan Markle makes her first appearance since ITV documentary as she attends youth summit without Prince Harry - just hours after 'royal' source told US TV how the couple had 'single-handedly modernised the monarchy'</a>
</h2>
<a href="/femail/article-7601407/Meghan-Markle-arrives-public-appearance-ITV-documentary-Royal-Albert-Hall.html">
<img data-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/2682937599988249088/308x174_JPG_2682937599988249088.jpg" data-fps="7" data-frame-count="51" data-frame-height="184" data-frame-width="308" data-hd-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/2682937599988249088/636x382_JPG-HD_2682937599988249088.jpg" data-hd-fps="7" data-hd-frame-count="51" data-hd-frame-height="382" data-hd-frame-width="636" data-mol-fe-video-preview="" data-src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/2682937599988249088/636x382_JPG-SINGLE_2682937599988249088.jpg" data-video="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/2682937599988249088/636x382_MP4_2682937599988249088.mp4" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>

</img></noscript>
</a>
<div class="articletext">
<div>
<p>
       
       The Royal Albert Hall event in Kensington, London was Meghan's first public appearance since an explosive ITV documentary in which she revealed scrutiny and media attention had left her feeling 'vulnerable'. It also came just hours after a source close to her and Prince Harry told CNN the couple are 'single-handedly' modernising the monarchy. Meghan, 38, attended without Harry, in her role as vice-president of the Queen's Commonwealth Trust, which is partnering with One Young World to bring 53 young leaders to the summit. The Duchess looked in high spirits as she arrived, wearing a purple dress and sporting a broad smile as admirers rushed to get photos (pictured left).

<img alt="RICHARD KAY: I fear Harry has failed to learn the lessons of his parents' public feud" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/22/22/20056298-0-image-a-87_1571779206802.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>

</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/debate/article-7601545/JANET-STREET-PORTER-Cher-Lady-Glenconner-teach-Harry-Meghan-thing-two.html">
<img alt="JANET STREET-PORTER: Cher and Lady Glenconner could teach Harry and Meghan a thing or two" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/22/18/20047200-0-image-a-26_1571764738672.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>

</img></noscript>

<div>
<p>
            
            JANET STREET PORTER: Harry and Meghan (pictured together left) are said to be taking a six-week' break' from Royal duties after their tour of Africa. Harry seems to feel it helps to tell us how he's feeling, but there's a danger that being so open could backfire. After all, many older people (who respect the monarchy and are big fans of Harry and Meghan) just got on with their lives through wars and deprivation, through loss and heartbreak. Luckily there are oldies like Cher (top right) and Lady Anne Glenconner (bottom right) to keep our spirits up. I wonder what Cher and Anne Glenconner would make of Harry and Meghan's troubled relationship with the world at large. I am sure their advice would be the same. Just grit your teeth and get on with it, darlings. (Janet Street-Porter, pictured bottom centre)

<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/femail/article-7601019/Mother-left-husband-cured-illnesses-finding-shamanic-energy.html" itemprop="url">Former investment banker who left her husband and 'cured her illnesses' after a chance meeting with a Shaman now helps business leaders make MILLIONS by putting them into trances</a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/femail/article-7601019/Mother-left-husband-cured-illnesses-finding-shamanic-energy.html">
<img alt="Mother left husband and 'cured her illnesses' after finding 'shamanic energy'" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/09/article-7601019-20074104-734_636x382.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="Mother left husband and 'cured her illnesses' after finding 'shamanic energy'" height="382" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/09/article-7601019-20074104-734_636x382.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div>
<p>
            
            Mother Sarah Negus, 52, from Surrey, pictured left and right, found the strength to cure her illnesses and leave her husband (top inset) after a chance meeting with a Shaman. The pair then travelled to Peru together where she learnt from indigenous Shamans in the Amazon and tried hallucinogenic tea, before returning to the UK and setting up her business offering 12-week courses costing £7,500. Describing her first meeting with her Shaman, Sarah said she was told everything she was afraid of and cried for two days.
        </p>

<div class="article article-tri-headline" itemprop="itemListElement" itemscope="" itemtype="//schema.org/ListItem">
<meta content="43" itemprop="position"/>
<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/sport/sportsnews/article-7602831/London-Paralympics-gold-medallist-Marieke-Vervoort-dies-euthanasia-aged-40.html" itemprop="url">London Paralympics gold medallist Marieke Vervoort dies through euthanasia aged 40 after battling degenerative disease that left her in agony</a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/sport/sportsnews/article-7602831/London-Paralympics-gold-medallist-Marieke-Vervoort-dies-euthanasia-aged-40.html">
<img alt="London Paralympics gold medallist Marieke Vervoort dies through euthanasia aged 40" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/02/20063588-0-image-m-11_1571792399040.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="London Paralympics gold medallist Marieke Vervoort dies through euthanasia aged 40" height="382" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/02/20063588-0-image-m-11_1571792399040.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div>
<p>
            
            Belgian wheelchair sprinter Marieke Vervoort (left, in a hospital bed with her beloved dog Zenn) suffered with constant pain, seizures and paralysis in her legs, and could barely sleep. The inspirational athlete (top right during the London Paralympics in 2012, and bottom right in Doha, Qatar, in 2015) signed paperwork in 2008 that would allow a doctor to end her life in the future, in her home country where assisted dying is legal. She stunned the world in 2016 when she announced she was considering killing herself after the Rio Games, in which she won a further two medals. After, she said she did not plan to die immediately but when her 'bad days outnumber the good.' A statement from Diest - where she was from near Brussels - simply said Vervoort 'responded to her choice on Tuesday evening'. She won gold in the T52 100m wheelchair race at London 2012 and silver in the T52 200m wheelchair race. She also won medals at the 2016 Rio Olympics, claiming silver in the T51/52 400m and bronze in the T51/52 100m.
        </p>
</div>
</div>

</h2>
<div class="articletext">

<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/tvshowbiz/article-7602633/Red-Hot-Chili-Peppers-bassist-Flea-57-weds-designer-Melody-Ehsani-39-LA.html" itemprop="url">Red Hot Chili Peppers bassist Flea, 57, weds designer Melody Ehsani, 39, in LA a month after engagement party</a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/tvshowbiz/article-7602633/Red-Hot-Chili-Peppers-bassist-Flea-57-weds-designer-Melody-Ehsani-39-LA.html">
<img alt="Red Hot Chili Peppers bassist Flea, 57, weds designer Melody Ehsani, 39, in LA" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/00/20061038-0-image-m-5_1571787287935.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>

</img></noscript>
</a>
<div>
<p>
            
            Red Hot Chili Peppers bassist Flea announced Tuesday that he'd married his second wife, designer Melody Ehsani, in Los Angeles on Sunday. At 57, the flamboyant rock star is 18 years older than the UCLA grad, who was born and raised in Los Angeles to Persian artist parents. The blushing bride called her glowing groom her 'best friend' and gushed: 'Surely, my best collaboration to date. You're my whole heart.'
        </p>

<div class="article article-tri-headline">
<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/femail/article-7601233/Viewers-slam-Gogglebox-star-Scarlett-Moffatts-exploitative-new-reality-show.html">'This show makes me sooo uncomfortable': Viewers slam Gogglebox star Scarlett Moffatt's 'exploitative' new reality show that sees her Durham home recreated alongside cattle-herders in remote Namibian village</a>
</h2>
<a href="/femail/article-7601233/Viewers-slam-Gogglebox-star-Scarlett-Moffatts-exploitative-new-reality-show.html">
<img data-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/6402998114825975964/308x174_JPG_6402998114825975964.jpg" data-fps="7" data-frame-count="62" data-frame-height="184" data-frame-width="308" data-hd-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/6402998114825975964/636x382_JPG-HD_6402998114825975964.jpg" data-hd-fps="7" data-hd-frame-count="62" data-hd-frame-height="382" data-hd-frame-width="636" data-mol-fe-video-preview="" data-src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/6402998114825975964/636x382_JPG-SINGLE_6402998114825975964.jpg" data-video="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/6402998114825975964/636x382_MP4_6402998114825975964.mp4" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img height="382" src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/6402998114825975964/636x382_JPG-SINGLE_6402998114825975964.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div class="articletext">
<div>
<p>
       
       The British Tribe Next Door sees Scarlett Moffatt and her family move into an exact replica of their County Durham home in Otjeme, a remote village in Namibia, complete with running water, electricity and wardrobes full of clothes - as well as WiFi. The Moffatts are living alongside the semi-nomadic Himba tribe of cattle herders for a month in the show. However, it has been slammed on social media, with viewers criticising Channel 4 for green-lighting it. It was also branded exploitative, with one Twitter user commenting: 'What the hell are Channel 4 execs thinking?The British Tribe next door is a shockingly awful idea -reality TV at its worst. It smacks of colonialism &amp; cultural exploitation &amp; will probably destroy the group who took part. Utterly irresponsible.'
     </p>
</div>
</div>

</h2>
<div class="articletext">

<noscript>

<p>
<span class="tag-new" data-article-id="7603871">NEW</span>

        Lady Millicent Hawes, 70, met Karl Buchberger on a trip to Vienna in 1938 - and helped raise money to help his family, including his Jewish wife Mitzi, escape to Canada. Pictured: The duchess.

</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/femail/article-7603209/Mother-comes-clever-strategy-keeping-avocados-green-involves-FREEZING-them.html">
<img alt="Mother comes up with a clever strategy for keeping avocados green - and it involves" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/05/20067944-0-image-a-54_1571804223295.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>

            An Australian woman has shared a nifty trick that keeps your avocados ripe until you're ready to eat them. 'This is a little trick I've been doing with avocado,' she said. 'I heard that you can buy it frozen but was never able to find it anywhere. So I thought why not freeze it myself? It works amazingly well.'

<meta content="66" itemprop="position"/>
<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/femail/article-7601091/Woman-40-snares-family-friend-abused-age-seven-hiding-secret-recorder-bra.html" itemprop="url">Woman, 40, reveals how she snared a family friend who sexually abused her when she was seven by confronting him three decades later and hiding a secret recorder in her bra</a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/femail/article-7601091/Woman-40-snares-family-friend-abused-age-seven-hiding-secret-recorder-bra.html">
<img alt="Woman, 40, snares family friend who abused her age of seven by hiding a secret recorder in" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/22/17/20046314-0-image-a-138_1571763388881.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="Woman, 40, snares family friend who abused her age of seven by hiding a secret recorder in" height="382" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/22/17/20046314-0-image-a-138_1571763388881.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div>
<p>
            
            EXCLUSIVE: A woman who was sexually abused by a family friend at the age of seven has told how she confronted the paedophile 30 years later - and snared him by hiding a recorder in her bra. Sam Welch, now 40, of Crawley, West Sussex, was repeatedly attacked by Clive Manning, now 54, pictured inset, in an annex on the grounds of her family home in Kent. After keeping quiet about it for three decades, Sam (pictured left as a child and right now) bumped into Manning by chance and confronted him about the abuse he inflicted. While he didn't confess, Sam recorded their conversation on a secret device which proved enough for a case to be built against him. In January this year, Manning was convicted of 12 historic counts of indecent assault and jailed for 15 years. Bravely waiving her right to anonymity, Sam said she finally feels 'free' of her past.
        </p>
</div>

<div class="article article-tri-headline" itemprop="itemListElement" itemscope="" itemtype="//schema.org/ListItem">
<meta content="69" itemprop="position"/>
<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/femail/article-7601569/Unhealthy-family-four-shed-6-5-stone-six-months-losing-grandmother-diabetes.html" itemprop="url">The family who ALL got ripped: Couple and their two sons embark on an incredible weightloss journey after losing their grandmother to diabetes - and shed 6.5 STONE in six months </a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/femail/article-7601569/Unhealthy-family-four-shed-6-5-stone-six-months-losing-grandmother-diabetes.html">
<img alt="Family-of-four lose 6.5 stones after grandmother's diabetes death" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/22/18/20047572-0-image-a-331_1571765264482.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="Family-of-four lose 6.5 stones after grandmother's diabetes death" height="382" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/22/18/20047572-0-image-a-331_1571765264482.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div>
<p>
            
            Sue McGillivbray, 51, from Manchester, overhauled the diet and exercise routines of husband Roy, 51 and sons Simon, 28 and Ben, 24, after losing her mother to type 2 diabetes. The foursome lost a combined total of 6.5 stone in six months. Sue said she had fallen out of love with herself prior to starting the diet, but starting to exercise again helped her regain her self-esteem. Pictured: the family's before and after transformation; from left: Sue, Simon, Roy and Ben.
        </p>

<h6>
      TURKEY ATTACKS SYRIA
  </h6>
<div> </div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="article article-tri-headline" itemprop="itemListElement" itemscope="" itemtype="//schema.org/ListItem">
<meta content="84" itemprop="position"/>
<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/news/article-7603981/So-ceasefire-Syrians-suffer-horrific-burns-border-town-explosion.html" itemprop="url">So much for a ceasefire: Syrians suffer horrific burns in border town explosion despite Turkey's claims it has stopped its offensive - as Russia says America betrayed the Kurds who will now be 'mauled' if they do not withdraw</a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/news/article-7603981/So-ceasefire-Syrians-suffer-horrific-burns-border-town-explosion.html">
<img alt="So much for a ceasefire: Syrians suffer horrific burns in border town explosion" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/10/20076080-0-image-a-2_1571822887557.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="So much for a ceasefire: Syrians suffer horrific burns in border town explosion" height="382" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/10/20076080-0-image-a-2_1571822887557.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div>
<p>
            
            WARNING: GRAPHIC CONTENT - Patients were seen with horrific burns on their faces and bodies (pictured above) at a hospital in northern Syria while a distressed young girl was carried with a badly injured leg after the mysterious blast in Ras al-Ain. Turkey said there was 'no need' to resume its operations when a five-day ceasefire ended last night. Russia today warned the Kurds would be 'mauled' if they do not withdraw from the border zone in Syria as the Kremlin savaged Washington for 'betraying' the Kurdish people. Vladimir Putin's spokesman said the 'Turkish military machine' would overpower any remaining Kurds and warned that Russian military police could not help them if they stayed in the border zone. Under a deal struck between Putin and Turkish leader Recep Tayyip Erdogan yesterday, Russian forces will work with Syrian border guards to patrol the border zone.
        </p>
</div>
</div>

<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/femail/article-7604155/Stomach-churning-pictures-reveal-horrors-living-Britains-worst-flatmates.html" itemprop="url">Partners in grime! Stomach-churning pictures reveal the horror of living with housemates who hate cleaning- from filth-encrusted toilets to dishes crawling with mould </a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/femail/article-7604155/Stomach-churning-pictures-reveal-horrors-living-Britains-worst-flatmates.html">
<img alt="Stomach-churning pictures reveal the horrors of living with Britain's worst flatmates " data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/10/20075558-0-image-a-54_1571821916329.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="Stomach-churning pictures reveal the horrors of living with Britain's worst flatmates " height="382" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/10/20075558-0-image-a-54_1571821916329.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div>
<p>
<span class="tag-new" data-article-id="7604155">NEW</span>

            Reddit users, from all over the world, have shared the eye-opening antics of their awful flatmates in an astonishing gallery. The pictures show fur and mould actually growing on leftover pasta and sinks so dirty that it can barely be scraped away. Other photos show bedroom with clothes strewn across the floor and another room with beer cans and cigarettes lining the carpet. l-r clockwise: Dirty dishes in a sink, a mould crawling dish, a room with clothes all over the floor, another room with beer cans and cigarettes, a dirty sink and lots of tangled wires
        </p>

<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/news/article-7603325/Hong-Kong-government-withdraw-bill-sparked-protests.html" itemprop="url">Hong Kong government withdraws extradition bill that sparked months of chaotic protests on the same day murder suspect who was the catalyst for the plan is released from jail</a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/news/article-7603325/Hong-Kong-government-withdraw-bill-sparked-protests.html">
<img alt="" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/08/20070070-0-Hong_Konger_Chan_Tong_kai_is_wanted_in_Taiwan_for_the_2018_murde-a-7_1571816491795.jpg" height="115" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="154"/>

</img></noscript>
</a>
<p>
        
        Months of pro-democracy demonstrations in the financial hub were sparked by protests against the now-cancelled extradition law - which would have allowed suspects to be extradited to China.

<h2 class="linkro-darkred">

<div class="article article-tri-headline">
<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/femail/article-7601053/Woman-wears-hidden-camera-catch-people-checking-cleavage.html">Caught you looking! Woman wears a hidden camera on her CHEST to catch strangers checking out her cleavage - but it's all to raise awareness for breast cancer and self-exams </a>
</h2>
<a href="/femail/article-7601053/Woman-wears-hidden-camera-catch-people-checking-cleavage.html">
<img data-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/9143484865329541537/308x174_JPG_9143484865329541537.jpg" data-fps="7" data-frame-count="70" data-frame-height="184" data-frame-width="308" data-hd-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/9143484865329541537/636x382_JPG-HD_9143484865329541537.jpg" data-hd-fps="7" data-hd-frame-count="70" data-hd-frame-height="382" data-hd-frame-width="636" data-mol-fe-video-preview="" data-src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/9143484865329541537/636x382_JPG-SINGLE_9143484865329541537.jpg" data-video="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/9143484865329541537/636x382_MP4_9143484865329541537.mp4" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img height="382" src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/9143484865329541537/636x382_JPG-SINGLE_9143484865329541537.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div class="articletext">
<div>
<p>
       
       Whitney Zelig, 29, from New York City, hid a small camera in her low-cut tank top before taking a walk through Manhattan with her cleavage on show. In the clip, she gets plenty of stares from men, women, and even a dog. Whitney's mother is a breast cancer survivor, and she made the montage with her brother, Chris Zelig, and their best friend CJ Koegel. The hilarious video was created to raise awareness for breast cancer and early detection through self-exams.
     </p>
</div>
</div>

<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/news/article-7604001/Teenager-demolishes-monster-TEN-PATTY-burger-cheese-20-minutes.html" itemprop="url">Teenager demolishes a monster TEN-PATTY burger (with cheese) in 20 minutes as Whataburger staff and diners cheer and chant his name </a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/news/article-7604001/Teenager-demolishes-monster-TEN-PATTY-burger-cheese-20-minutes.html">
<img alt="" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/10/20074832-0-image-m-31_1571822602400.jpg" height="115" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="154"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="" height="115" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/10/20074832-0-image-m-31_1571822602400.jpg" width="154">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<p>
<span class="tag-new" data-article-id="7604001">NEW</span>

        Ethan Mueller, 17, ate the gargantuan meal at a Whataburger in Allen, Collin County, Texas last week. He had told friends he was going to beat the record of seven patties.
    </p>

<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/sciencetech/article-7599811/Pig-tailed-macaques-work-pest-control-chasing-EATING-rodents.html" itemprop="url">Stunned scientists discover rat-eating MONKEYS in Malaysia: Pig-tailed macaques are working as 'pest control' on palm oil plantations by chasing down and gobbling up rodents</a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/sciencetech/article-7599811/Pig-tailed-macaques-work-pest-control-chasing-EATING-rodents.html">
<img alt="" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/22/10/20028584-0-image-m-40_1571736841613.jpg" height="115" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="154"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="" height="115" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/22/10/20028584-0-image-m-40_1571736841613.jpg" width="154">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<p>
        
        Zoologists led fromthe Universiti Sains Malaysia have been studying the ecology and behaviour of Southern pig-tailed macaques in the Segari Melintang Forest Reserve in Malaysia since 2016.
    </p>

<div class="article article-tri-headline" itemprop="itemListElement" itemscope="" itemtype="//schema.org/ListItem">
<meta content="97" itemprop="position"/>
<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/news/article-7601719/PICTURED-Inside-Trump-Winery-wedding-conservative-activist-Candace-Owens.html" itemprop="url">A meeting of right minds: American conservative black commentator Candace Owens marries British Tory peer's son after whirlwind eight-month romance - with Nigel Farage as guest of honour</a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/news/article-7601719/PICTURED-Inside-Trump-Winery-wedding-conservative-activist-Candace-Owens.html">
<img alt="PICTURED: Inside the Trump Winery wedding of conservative activist Candace Owens" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/06/20069846-0-image-a-8_1571808679061.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="PICTURED: Inside the Trump Winery wedding of conservative activist Candace Owens" height="382" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/06/20069846-0-image-a-8_1571808679061.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div>
<p>
            
            New details and photographs have emerged of conservative commentator Candace Owens' wedding to British Oxford graduate George Farmer at Trump Winery in Virginia, revealing the bride wore three gowns and their guests donned mock red MAGA hats that had 'Make Weddings Great Again' written on them. The 30-year-old political activist is a well-known supporter of the President, who once praised her for being 'tough' and 'someone I admire'. Her controversial views have been backed by Kanye West, who once tweeted: 'I love the way Candace Owens thinks'. Owens met Farmer in December 2018 at the launch of Turning Point UK - a conservative youth organization. She was the former communications director for the US branch. The couple were engaged over a FaceTime call just two-and-a-half weeks after meeting. They topped off their whirlwind romance with the lavish wedding over Labor Weekend in Virginia, where conservative guests included Jon Voigt, conservative radio host Larry Elder, Brexit Party leader Nigel Farage, bottom right, and Charlie Kirk, who founded right-wing Turning Point USA. The couple have now publicly thanked Eric Trump - the president of Trump Winery - for the 'incredible venue and. As much as Trump firebrand Owens has courted controversy since bursting onto the political scene, so has her husband. Farmer, whose father is British baron and conservative peer Lord Michael Farmer, is equally as controversial and was a candidate for the Brexit Party in Britain but didn't win a seat in the European Parliamentary elections. The couple are pictured top right with the president.
        </p>
</div>

<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/news/article-7603789/Appalling-moment-shameless-shopper-makes-charity-collection-box.html">Appalling moment a shameless shopper makes off with the charity collection box from a store counter helped by her friend</a>
</h2>
<a href="/news/article-7603789/Appalling-moment-shameless-shopper-makes-charity-collection-box.html">
<img data-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/4097731570747394682/308x174_JPG_4097731570747394682.jpg" data-fps="7" data-frame-count="56" data-frame-height="270" data-frame-width="308" data-hd-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/4097731570747394682/636x382_JPG-HD_4097731570747394682.jpg" data-hd-fps="7" data-hd-frame-count="56" data-hd-frame-height="560" data-hd-frame-width="636" data-mol-fe-video-preview="" data-src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/4097731570747394682/636x382_JPG-SINGLE_4097731570747394682.jpg" data-video="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/4097731570747394682/636x382_MP4_4097731570747394682.mp4" height="560" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>

</img></noscript>
</a>
<div class="articletext">
<div>
<p>
       
       The shameless pair of women stole from the Londis in Kentstown, County Meath, which was raising money for a charity which carries out river rescues. The Garda - Irish police - has seen the footage and is now trying to track down the two women. When the cashier was distracted, one of the women wrenched out the chain attaching the tin to the counter and quickly slipped it under her jacket before both she and her accomplice made a hasty exit.
     </p>

<div class="article article-tri-headline" itemprop="itemListElement" itemscope="" itemtype="//schema.org/ListItem">
<meta content="107" itemprop="position"/>
<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/news/article-7602041/Its-perfect-year-fungi-foragers-dodge-killers-cropping-woodlands.html" itemprop="url">A bumper harvest of DEATH: It's a perfect year for fungi foragers... if you can dodge the killers cropping up in abundance in Britain's woodlands </a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/news/article-7602041/Its-perfect-year-fungi-foragers-dodge-killers-cropping-woodlands.html">
<img alt="It's a perfect year for fungi foragers... if you can dodge the killers cropping up in" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/22/22/20057284-0-image-a-51_1571780857481.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="It's a perfect year for fungi foragers... if you can dodge the killers cropping up in" height="382" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/22/22/20057284-0-image-a-51_1571780857481.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div>
<p>
            
            Hours of autumn sunshine after months of rain means that fields and woods are full of numerous varieties of mushroom. It's the perfect weather for foraging. The golden rule with mushrooms, of course, is never to eat them unless you are completely confident that what you've found is edible. My advice, then, if you're inspired to go in search of these natural wonders is look but don't eat. For if the fungi on the right are anything to go by, pick the wrong mushroom and it could be your last.
        </p>
</div>

</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/news/article-7602101/Abominable-vicar-stole-5-000-96-year-old-parishioner-jailed-10-months.html">
<img alt="" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/22/21/20054094-0-image-m-78_1571777581474.jpg" height="115" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="154"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="" height="115" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/22/21/20054094-0-image-m-78_1571777581474.jpg" width="154">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<p>
        
        Reverend Edward Morris, from Nottingham, spent the money on 'books and fripperies' after widower Joan Pennack made him a signatory on her bank account when she went into care home.

<div class="article article-tri-headline" itemprop="itemListElement" itemscope="" itemtype="//schema.org/ListItem">
<meta content="119" itemprop="position"/>
<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/travel/travel_news/article-7596687/Michael-Polizas-new-book-World-teNeues-treasure-chest-landscape-photography.html" itemprop="url">A treasure chest of incredible images: Astonishing coffee table book will take you on a photographic world tour across all seven continents</a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/travel/travel_news/article-7596687/Michael-Polizas-new-book-World-teNeues-treasure-chest-landscape-photography.html">
<img alt="Michael Poliza's new book The World by teNeues is a treasure chest of landscape" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/09/20073830-0-image-a-28_1571818767394.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="Michael Poliza's new book The World by teNeues is a treasure chest of landscape" height="382" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/09/20073830-0-image-a-28_1571818767394.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div>
<p>
            
            Want to tour the world's most beautiful landscapes? Then feast your eyes on these stunning pictures. They were captured by seasoned globetrotter and photographer Michael Poliza , from Germany, who has travelled through more than 170 countries, always on the lookout for breathtaking landscapes, remote regions and pristine nature reserves. And they all feature in his brand new book, The World (by teNeues ), which is a treasure chest of his unpublished images from all seven continents. The book isn't cheap. It costs £225 (250 euros/$300) for the unsigned 'special edition' and £850 (999 euros/$1200) for the hand-signed 'collector's edition'. But it contains a sensational body of work. Pictured is a moss-covered volcano in Iceland, birds on the remote Kuril Islands, Australia's bizarre Bungle Bungles rock formations and a mangrove forest in Thailand.
        </p>
</div>
</div>

<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/femail/article-7603983/Obese-woman-shed-whopping-eight-stone-dubbed-Gemma-Collins-nights-out.html" itemprop="url">Obese woman who men dubbed 'Gemma Collins' - sheds six stone by ditching takeaways and the 20 vodka and Red Bulls she used to drink on nights out</a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/femail/article-7603983/Obese-woman-shed-whopping-eight-stone-dubbed-Gemma-Collins-nights-out.html">
<img alt="Obese woman shed a whopping eight stone after being dubbed 'Gemma Collins' on nights out " data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/09/20074022-0-image-a-18_1571819049636.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="Obese woman shed a whopping eight stone after being dubbed 'Gemma Collins' on nights out " height="382" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/09/20074022-0-image-a-18_1571819049636.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div>
<p>
            
            Chrystal-Sky Dawson, 23, from Tewkesbury, Gloucestershire, was left mortified when strangers compared her to The Only Way Is Essex star, Gemma Collins, thanks to her long blonde hair and love of glittery clothing. When her weight ballooned to  21st (left) the student decided to swap greasy food for healthy meals and lost a whopping eight stone and now weighs a slender 13st 4lbs (right).
        </p>

<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/femail/article-7599771/Teen-girls-branded-awe-inspiring-dancing-abusive-voicemails-exes-TikTok.html">Teenage girls are branded 'awe-inspiring' for dancing to grovelling voicemails from their exes in new TikTok trend</a>
</h2>
<a href="/femail/article-7599771/Teen-girls-branded-awe-inspiring-dancing-abusive-voicemails-exes-TikTok.html">
<img data-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/64545702455561725/308x174_JPG_64545702455561725.jpg" data-fps="7" data-frame-count="60" data-frame-height="184" data-frame-width="308" data-hd-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/64545702455561725/636x382_JPG-HD_64545702455561725.jpg" data-hd-fps="7" data-hd-frame-count="60" data-hd-frame-height="382" data-hd-frame-width="636" data-mol-fe-video-preview="" data-src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/64545702455561725/636x382_JPG-SINGLE_64545702455561725.jpg" data-video="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/64545702455561725/636x382_MP4_64545702455561725.mp4" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>

</img></noscript>
</a>
<div class="articletext">
<div>
<p>
       
       The craze is believed to have been started by an American teenager called Isabella, 18, who shared a video of herself dancing to a grovelling apology from her cheating ex, begging her to trust him. It directly inspired Tenley Earles, 18, from Virginia, who's posted an interpretive dance of herself moving to a audio of her ex-boyfriend, a pastor's son, berating her for wearing leggings to school.

<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/news/article-7603905/New-constable-20-joins-mother-stepfather-stepsister-police.html" itemprop="url">Keeping it in the family! New constable, 20, joins her mother, stepfather AND stepsister in the police</a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/news/article-7603905/New-constable-20-joins-mother-stepfather-stepsister-police.html">
<img alt="New constable, 20, joins her mother, stepfather AND stepsister in the police" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/10/20073862-0-image-a-4_1571821296949.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="New constable, 20, joins her mother, stepfather AND stepsister in the police" height="382" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/10/20073862-0-image-a-4_1571821296949.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div>
<p>
            
            A police family have welcomed their second daughter to the force they work for, as they celebrate two generations of police officers in the bloodline. Lauren Jackson, 20, (middle right) attested as a police constable in August, joining not one but three members of her family who are also currently serving officers in Greater Manchester Police force. The new recruit is following the footsteps of her mum, Tina, (far right) a Detective Constable in Bury, stepdad, Inspector Jim Jones (far left) who is part of the Criminal Justice Branch, and stepsister, Rachel, a PC in Tameside (middle left). Lauren will be joining the team in Rochdale.
        </p>
</div>

<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/femail/article-7603769/Woman-24-marries-poignant-ceremony-diagnosed-terminal-cancer.html" itemprop="url">Dying woman, 24, who is one of the youngest in the UK to be diagnosed with terminal breast cancer fulfills her dream of marrying</a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/femail/article-7603769/Woman-24-marries-poignant-ceremony-diagnosed-terminal-cancer.html">
<img alt="Woman, 24, marries in a poignant ceremony after being diagnosed with terminal cancer" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/09/20073706-0-image-a-68_1571819238839.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="Woman, 24, marries in a poignant ceremony after being diagnosed with terminal cancer" height="382" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/09/20073706-0-image-a-68_1571819238839.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div>
<p>
            
            Vicki Eastaugh, from Hertfordshire, vowed to love her husband Simon, 25, 'till death us do part', and said they refused to let negativity and fears about the future surface on their big day. Seven months ago Vicki was told she had stage four breast cancer and, while she was not given a precise life expectancy, doctors said there was nothing more they could do to help her. Vicki was first told she had the disease at the age of 21 in November 2016, and underwent a double mastectomy and six rounds of chemotherapy before her treatment (inset) ended in June 2017.
        </p>
</div>

<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/news/article-7603861/Embarrassing-moment-PC-smashes-patrol-car-traffic-lights-outside-police-station.html">It's a fair cop! Embarrassing moment PC smashes his patrol car into traffic lights outside police station as he overtakes colleague on a bicycle</a>
</h2>
<a href="/news/article-7603861/Embarrassing-moment-PC-smashes-patrol-car-traffic-lights-outside-police-station.html">
<img data-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/6647469727156712187/308x174_JPG_6647469727156712187.jpg" data-fps="7" data-frame-count="41" data-frame-height="184" data-frame-width="308" data-hd-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/6647469727156712187/636x382_JPG-HD_6647469727156712187.jpg" data-hd-fps="7" data-hd-frame-count="41" data-hd-frame-height="380" data-hd-frame-width="636" data-mol-fe-video-preview="" data-src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/6647469727156712187/636x382_JPG-SINGLE_6647469727156712187.jpg" data-video="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/6647469727156712187/636x382_MP4_6647469727156712187.mp4" height="380" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img height="380" src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/23/6647469727156712187/636x382_JPG-SINGLE_6647469727156712187.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div class="articletext">
<div>
<p>
       
       PC Mark Chance, 25, caused thousands of pound of damage when he smashed into the lights in central Perth, Scotland on May 1 this year (pictured, CCTV of the crash). The local authority was left with a £3,000 bill for the destroyed lights and the installation of temporary traffic lights following the smash. A Police Scotland spokesperson said: 'We are aware of the outcome of the case and a report will be made to Deputy Chief Constable for People and Professionalism Fiona Taylor for consideration of misconduct proceedings.' At Perth Sheriff Court, Chance admitted driving carelessly and was fined £600 and had six penalty points added to his licence.
     </p>
</div>
</div>

<div class="article article-tri-headline" itemprop="itemListElement" itemscope="" itemtype="//schema.org/ListItem">
<meta content="154" itemprop="position"/>
<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/femail/article-7600903/From-London-garage-cork-house-Kevin-McCloud-reveals-contenders-House-Year.html" itemprop="url">From a London garage transformed into a three-storey property to a home made from cork that's so light it could float away, these are the 'experimental' contenders for Grand Designs House of the Year </a>
</h2>
<div class="articletext">
<a href="/femail/article-7600903/From-London-garage-cork-house-Kevin-McCloud-reveals-contenders-House-Year.html">
<img alt="From a London garage to a cork house, Kevin McCloud reveals contenders for House of the" data-src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/00/20061040-0-image-m-4_1571787137994.jpg" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img alt="From a London garage to a cork house, Kevin McCloud reveals contenders for House of the" height="382" src="https://i.dailymail.co.uk/1s/2019/10/23/00/20061040-0-image-m-4_1571787137994.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div>
<p>
            
            Kevin McCloud is set to reveal five experimental homes in the running for the 2019 Royal Institute of British Architects House of the Year on Grand Designs tonight. Each week he'll exclusively reveal which houses the RIBA judges have put through to the shortlist, and in the final programme announce the winner of this prestigious prize. (Pictured clockwise, from top left: a home made from cork in Berkshire; a converted garage in London; an eco-home in the Chiltern Hills; a modern day castle in Sussex; and inset, a contemporary cottage in Derbyshire).
        </p>
</div>
</div>

<h2 class="linkro-darkred">

</h2>
<a href="/news/article-7601859/Diver-underwater-tug-o-war-octopus-tries-steal-camera.html">
<img data-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/7098766502057298407/308x174_JPG_7098766502057298407.jpg" data-fps="7" data-frame-count="70" data-frame-height="184" data-frame-width="308" data-hd-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/7098766502057298407/636x382_JPG-HD_7098766502057298407.jpg" data-hd-fps="7" data-hd-frame-count="70" data-hd-frame-height="382" data-hd-frame-width="636" data-mol-fe-video-preview="" data-src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/7098766502057298407/636x382_JPG-SINGLE_7098766502057298407.jpg" data-video="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/7098766502057298407/636x382_MP4_7098766502057298407.mp4" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>

</a>
<div class="articletext">
<div>
<p>
       
       The clip, filmed by Eric Desmet in June, captures a young octopus as it grabs onto a camera which had been set up to record the cephalopod as it hid in a small cave. When Mr Desmet attempts to remove the device from the grasp of the octopus, it fights back, pulling with its eight arms to hold onto the camera.
     </p>

<div class="article article-tri-headline">
<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/news/article-7601541/Ten-day-old-Billie-pulls-adorable-expressions-father-massages-head.html">Baby bliss! Ten-day-old Billie pulls the most adorable expressions when her father massages her head </a>
</h2>
<a href="/news/article-7601541/Ten-day-old-Billie-pulls-adorable-expressions-father-massages-head.html">
<img data-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/7239635428205871412/308x174_JPG_7239635428205871412.jpg" data-fps="7" data-frame-count="70" data-frame-height="184" data-frame-width="308" data-hd-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/7239635428205871412/636x382_JPG-HD_7239635428205871412.jpg" data-hd-fps="7" data-hd-frame-count="70" data-hd-frame-height="382" data-hd-frame-width="636" data-mol-fe-video-preview="" data-src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/7239635428205871412/636x382_JPG-SINGLE_7239635428205871412.jpg" data-video="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/7239635428205871412/636x382_MP4_7239635428205871412.mp4" height="382" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img height="382" src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/7239635428205871412/636x382_JPG-SINGLE_7239635428205871412.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div class="articletext">
<div>
<p>
       
       The video (left), posted to Facebook, captures Australian Josh Hawkins (right with his wife Cat and his daughter Billie) as he sits with his newborn daughter Billie while carefully rubbing his hand over the top of her hair. As he does, the youngster smiles up at him, slowly sticking out her tongue at her father as her eyes begin to droop with his movement. The clip has been viewed more than 8.2million times, with around 106,000 shares and 44,000 comments on Facebook.
     </p>

<div class="articletext">

<h2 class="linkro-darkred">
<a href="/news/article-7601339/Drone-choreographed-schoolchildren-breaks-Guinness-World-Record.html">BT breaks Guinness World Record by flying 160 synchronized drones in biggest ever indoor flight spectacular - all controlled by just a single computer</a>
</h2>
<a href="/news/article-7601339/Drone-choreographed-schoolchildren-breaks-Guinness-World-Record.html">
<img data-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/8446798725309025496/308x174_JPG_8446798725309025496.jpg" data-fps="7" data-frame-count="70" data-frame-height="184" data-frame-width="308" data-hd-film="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/8446798725309025496/636x382_JPG-HD_8446798725309025496.jpg" data-hd-fps="7" data-hd-frame-count="70" data-hd-frame-height="380" data-hd-frame-width="636" data-mol-fe-video-preview="" data-src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/8446798725309025496/636x382_JPG-SINGLE_8446798725309025496.jpg" data-video="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/8446798725309025496/636x382_MP4_8446798725309025496.mp4" height="380" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" width="636"/>
<noscript>
<img height="380" src="https://video.dailymail.co.uk/preview/mol/2019/10/22/8446798725309025496/636x382_JPG-SINGLE_8446798725309025496.jpg" width="636">
</img></noscript>
</a>
<div class="articletext">
<div>
<p>
       
       The event was held at Wembley by the telecommunications company BT and the use of 160 drones broke a record for the most to have been controlled by the same computer.
